# Skills · S · part 43

834 entries, ssc-fit-positioning to stakeholder-alignment. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ssc-fit-positioning**: Use when judging whether a manuscript is on-target for 《中国社会科学》 (Social Sciences in China) before investing in revision — screening for 思想分量 over technical sophistication, big-question level, cross-disciplinary reach, and original theory — and to recommend a better venue when fit is low. The most common save: stopping a niche empirical paper from being mis-submitted. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Social-Sciences-in-China-Skills/skills/ssc-fit-positioning) · ★1.1k)
- **ssc-literature-dialogue**: Use when building or fixing the literature review for 《中国社会科学》 (Social Sciences in China) — entering the theoretical lineage (classic authors and schools) rather than piling up recent empirical citations, and positioning the paper as a move within an ongoing scholarly conversation. Use when references are a variable-list instead of a theory dialogue. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Social-Sciences-in-China-Skills/skills/ssc-literature-dialogue) · ★1.1k)
- **ssc-rebuttal**: Use when responding to external-review (外审) comments from 《中国社会科学》 (Social Sciences in China) — drafting a point-by-point response letter that defends or strengthens the original proposition, deepens theory and cross-disciplinary dialogue, and revises the manuscript before writing replies. Use after revising the text, not before. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Social-Sciences-in-China-Skills/skills/ssc-rebuttal) · ★1.1k)
- **ssc-style**: Use when polishing the language and rhetoric of a 《中国社会科学》 (Social Sciences in China) manuscript — replacing policy-report tone and empty-significance phrases with scholarly argument, keeping a grand-but-restrained register, and ensuring paragraph-leading topic sentences. Use after the proposition and argument are settled. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Social-Sciences-in-China-Skills/skills/ssc-style) · ★1.1k)
- **ssc-submission**: Use when running the pre-submission preflight for 《中国社会科学》 (Social Sciences in China) — checking footnote (页下注) conventions, abstract/keyword limits, bilingual title/abstract, the online submission system (sscp.portal.founderss.cn), anonymity, and document hygiene. Use right before submitting; verify every rule against the journal's current 投稿须知. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Social-Sciences-in-China-Skills/skills/ssc-submission) · ★1.1k)
- **ssc-theory-contribution**: Use when articulating the original theoretical contribution for 《中国社会科学》 (Social Sciences in China) — turning empirical findings into a concept / proposition / analytical framework, and positioning the work within China's autonomous knowledge system (自主知识体系) rather than verifying imported theory. Use when the contribution still reads as "we find that X affects Y". ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Social-Sciences-in-China-Skills/skills/ssc-theory-contribution) · ★1.1k)
- **ssc-topic-problematic**: Use when framing or sharpening the research problem (问题意识 / problematic) for 《中国社会科学》 (Social Sciences in China) — elevating a topic from a narrow empirical question to a 重大理论与现实问题, locating the live scholarly controversy, and tying it to China's development / 时代之问. Use before writing the introduction. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Social-Sciences-in-China-Skills/skills/ssc-topic-problematic) · ★1.1k)

- **ssi-artifact-evaluation**: 当为《中国科学：信息科学》(Scientia Sinica Informationis, SSI) 稿件准备代码与数据可用性材料、需要判断本刊是否设有独立"制品评审/Artifact Evaluation"环节及如何据实处理时调用。本刊作为中文信息科学综合旗舰，并未采用英文会议式的独立制品徽章流程（现状：待核实/以官网当期为准），本技能说明如何在没有强制徽章制度的前提下，主动提供可用、可查、可复现的代码与数据，撰写诚实的可用性声明，并与英文姊妹刊 Science China Information Sciences 的开放科学要求对照。适用于计算机、控制、通信、微电子等各子学科稿件。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Scientia-Sinica-Informationis-Skills/skills/ssi-artifact-evaluation) · ★1.1k)
- **ssi-author-response**: 当收到《中国科学：信息科学》(Scientia Sinica Informationis, SSI) 外审意见、需要撰写审稿意见答复信与修回说明时调用。指导如何逐条回应每位审稿人与编委的意见、在修改稿中标注对应位置、以数据与理由而非情绪回应质疑、区分"已修改/已澄清/保留并说明"、处理相互矛盾的意见、控制修回轮次与时限，并保持中文学术礼节。适用于退修（大修/小修）后的 revise-and-resubmit 阶段，与英文姊妹刊 Science China Information Sciences 的修回习惯相通但以本刊中文流程为准。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Scientia-Sinica-Informationis-Skills/skills/ssi-author-response) · ★1.1k)
- **ssi-camera-ready**: 当稿件被《中国科学：信息科学》(Scientia Sinica Informationis, SSI) 录用、需要完成定稿、校样（清样）校对与最终提交时调用。覆盖录用后清样一校/二校、中英文摘要与作者信息/单位/基金的最终核对、参考文献与图表的清样级检查、版权协议与版面费缴纳、样刊、DOI 与卷期信息、以及与英文姊妹刊 Science China Information Sciences 的区分。适用于从"录用通知"到"正式见刊"之间的定稿阶段，确保清样与作者预期一致、零遗留错误。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Scientia-Sinica-Informationis-Skills/skills/ssi-camera-ready) · ★1.1k)
- **ssi-experiments**: 当需要为《中国科学：信息科学》(Scientia Sinica Informationis, SSI) 稿件设计与呈现实验、使证据与主张相称时调用。覆盖主张-证据匹配、真实数据与代表性基准、公平对比与统一评测协议、仿真/半实物/硅后实测/理论证明的区分、统计报告与显著性、消融实验隔离贡献、跨子学科（计算机/控制/通信/微电子）的证据范式，以及理论类论文的证明呈现。作为国家级大信息学科综合旗舰，本刊强调科学价值与严谨证据，实验设计需经得起外审推敲。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Scientia-Sinica-Informationis-Skills/skills/ssi-experiments) · ★1.1k)
- **ssi-related-work**: 当需要为《中国科学：信息科学》(Scientia Sinica Informationis, SSI) 稿件撰写相关工作与文献综述、明确本文与已有工作的差异（delta）时调用。覆盖差异优先的定位写法、国内外文献的均衡覆盖（不只引英文也不只引中文）、按技术脉络组织综述、评述栏目与论文相关工作节的不同要求、自引与利益关系处理、以及参考文献著录规范。作为国家级大信息学科综合旗舰，本刊看重创新高度，相关工作是论证"新在哪里"的关键，需客观、充分、有观点。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Scientia-Sinica-Informationis-Skills/skills/ssi-related-work) · ★1.1k)

- **ssi-workflow**: 当需要对一篇投往《中国科学：信息科学》(Scientia Sinica Informationis, SSI) 的稿件做从选题到见刊的全流程编排时调用。串联选题与栏目定位、中文写作、实验与可复现、相关工作、投稿审计、三审三校审稿、修回答复、录用定稿校样等阶段，给出各阶段的顺序、交付物、时间意识与所用技能。作为国家级大信息学科综合旗舰，本刊以重大原创与评述为主、强调科学价值与创新高度；本技能是把 12 个 SSI 技能编织起来的总控，并厘清与英文姊妹刊 Science China Information Sciences 的择一投稿关系。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Scientia-Sinica-Informationis-Skills/skills/ssi-workflow) · ★1.1k)
- **ssi-writing-style**: 当需要按《中国科学：信息科学》(Scientia Sinica Informationis, SSI) 的中文科技写作规范撰写或打磨稿件时调用。覆盖首页弧线（问题→现状不足→贡献→证据→意义）、中英文双语摘要与关键词、标题、量与单位、变量正斜体、图表与公式规范、参考文献著录（GB/T 7714 与否待核实）、中图分类号、基金著录、以及本刊强调的科学价值与创新高度的表达。作为国家级大信息学科综合旗舰，本刊要求中文表达严谨、英文摘要国际可读，与英文姊妹刊 Science China Information Sciences 并行。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Scientia-Sinica-Informationis-Skills/skills/ssi-writing-style) · ★1.1k)
